## custom resize: none
# C:\p\a.png
  [Convert] Convert
    convert.to-jpeg | To JPEG | writes True | suffix - | out Jpeg
    convert.to-webp | To WebP | writes True | suffix - | out WebP
    convert.to-bmp | To BMP | writes True | suffix - | out Bmp
    convert.to-tiff | To TIFF | writes True | suffix - | out Tiff
    convert.to-avif | To AVIF | writes True | suffix - | out Avif
  [Tools] Tools
    metadata.strip | Remove metadata | writes True | suffix no metadata | out Png
    metadata.info | Image info | writes False | suffix - | out Png
  [Compress] Compress
    compress.lossless | Lossless | writes True | suffix compressed | out Png
  [Resize] Resize
    resize.percent-25 | 25% | writes True | suffix resized 25% | out Png
    resize.percent-50 | 50% | writes True | suffix resized 50% | out Png
    resize.percent-75 | 75% | writes True | suffix resized 75% | out Png
    resize.box-1920x1080 | 1920×1080 | writes True | suffix 1920x1080 | out Png
    resize.box-1280x720 | 1280×720 | writes True | suffix 1280x720 | out Png
    resize.box-1080x1080 | 1080×1080 | writes True | suffix 1080x1080 | out Png
  [Crop] Crop
    crop.1x1 | 1:1 | writes True | suffix cropped 1x1 | out Png
    crop.4x3 | 4:3 | writes True | suffix cropped 4x3 | out Png
    crop.16x9 | 16:9 | writes True | suffix cropped 16x9 | out Png
    crop.9x16 | 9:16 | writes True | suffix cropped 9x16 | out Png
  [Rotate] Rotate
    rotate.left | Left | writes True | suffix rotated left | out Png
    rotate.right | Right | writes True | suffix rotated right | out Png
    rotate.180 | 180° | writes True | suffix rotated 180 | out Png
    flip.horizontal | Flip horizontal | writes True | suffix flipped horizontally | out Png
    flip.vertical | Flip vertical | writes True | suffix flipped vertically | out Png
  [Adjust] Adjust
    adjust.black-and-white | Black & white | writes True | suffix black and white | out Png
    adjust.brighter | Brighter | writes True | suffix brighter | out Png
    adjust.darker | Darker | writes True | suffix darker | out Png
    adjust.contrast | More contrast | writes True | suffix more contrast | out Png
    adjust.vivid | More vivid | writes True | suffix more vivid | out Png
    adjust.sharpen | Sharpen | writes True | suffix sharpened | out Png
    adjust.blur | Blur | writes True | suffix blurred | out Png

    convert.to-png | To PNG | writes True | suffix - | out Png
    convert.to-webp | To WebP | writes True | suffix - | out WebP
    convert.to-bmp | To BMP | writes True | suffix - | out Bmp
    convert.to-tiff | To TIFF | writes True | suffix - | out Tiff
    convert.to-avif | To AVIF | writes True | suffix - | out Avif
  [Tools] Tools
    metadata.strip | Remove metadata | writes True | suffix no metadata | out Jpeg
    metadata.info | Image info | writes False | suffix - | out Jpeg
  [Compress] Compress
    compress.high | High quality | writes True | suffix compressed | out Jpeg
    compress.balanced | Balanced | writes True | suffix compressed | out Jpeg
    compress.small | Smallest | writes True | suffix compressed | out Jpeg
  [Resize] Resize
    resize.percent-25 | 25% | writes True | suffix resized 25% | out Jpeg
    resize.percent-50 | 50% | writes True | suffix resized 50% | out Jpeg
    resize.percent-75 | 75% | writes True | suffix resized 75% | out Jpeg
    resize.box-1920x1080 | 1920×1080 | writes True | suffix 1920x1080 | out Jpeg
    resize.box-1280x720 | 1280×720 | writes True | suffix 1280x720 | out Jpeg
    resize.box-1080x1080 | 1080×1080 | writes True | suffix 1080x1080 | out Jpeg
    resize.box-1920x1080-stretch | 1920×1080 (stretch) | writes True | suffix 1920x1080 | out Jpeg
  [Crop] Crop
    crop.1x1 | 1:1 | writes True | suffix cropped 1x1 | out Jpeg
    crop.4x3 | 4:3 | writes True | suffix cropped 4x3 | out Jpeg
    crop.16x9 | 16:9 | writes True | suffix cropped 16x9 | out Jpeg
    crop.9x16 | 9:16 | writes True | suffix cropped 9x16 | out Jpeg
  [Rotate] Rotate
    rotate.left | Left | writes True | suffix rotated left | out Jpeg
    rotate.right | Right | writes True | suffix rotated right | out Jpeg
    rotate.180 | 180° | writes True | suffix rotated 180 | out Jpeg
    flip.horizontal | Flip horizontal | writes True | suffix flipped horizontally | out Jpeg
    flip.vertical | Flip vertical | writes True | suffix flipped vertically | out Jpeg
  [Adjust] Adjust
    adjust.black-and-white | Black & white | writes True | suffix black and white | out Jpeg
    adjust.brighter | Brighter | writes True | suffix brighter | out Jpeg
    adjust.darker | Darker | writes True | suffix darker | out Jpeg
    adjust.contrast | More contrast | writes True | suffix more contrast | out Jpeg
    adjust.vivid | More vivid | writes True | suffix more vivid | out Jpeg
    adjust.sharpen | Sharpen | writes True | suffix sharpened | out Jpeg
    adjust.blur | Blur | writes True | suffix blurred | out Jpeg
